{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport json\n\nwith open('/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-4/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-10-projects-provenance-weight-training/2a7be13a-c493-47d9-90af-377b3a1f6f4b/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1779638273939.txt', 'r') as f:\n    outer = json.load(f)\n\nregistry = json.loads(outer['result'])\n\n# Find all experiments matching the patterns\nimport re\n\ntargets = []\nfor exp_id, exp_data in registry.items():\n    if 'detect_remove' in exp_id or 'grid_b' in exp_id or 'permuted' in exp_id:\n        targets.append((exp_id, exp_data))\n\ntargets.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\nprint(f'Total matching experiments: {len(targets)}')\nprint('='*120)\n\nfor exp_id, data in targets:\n    print(f'exp_id: {exp_id}')\n    print(f'  status: {data.get(\\\"status\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")}')\n    print(f'  conclusion: {data.get(\\\"conclusion\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")}')\n    metrics = data.get('metrics', {})\n    if metrics:\n        print(f'  metrics: {json.dumps(metrics, indent=4)}')\n    else:\n        print(f'  metrics: (none)')\n    # Also check for gpu info\n    if 'gpu' in data:\n        print(f'  gpu: {data[\\\"gpu\\\"]}')\n    if 'server' in data:\n        print(f'  server: {data[\\\"server\\\"]}')\n    if 'config' in data:\n        config = data['config']\n        # Print relevant config params\n        relevant = {k:v for k,v in config.items() if k in ['beta', 'q', 'quantile', 'threshold', 'b', 'grid_b']}\n        if relevant:\n            print(f'  config_params: {relevant}')\n    print()\n\" 2>&1\n",
  "description": "Parse registry JSON and extract matching experiments",
  "timeout": 30000
}

This procedure is also sometimes referred to as \\\\u201cscaling & root planing\\\\u201d or \\\\u201cperiodontal therapy\\\\u201d.\\\\nIn periodontal disease, swollen unhealthy gums start to pull away from the teeth because of pockets where infection-causing plaque & tartar accumulate. The dentist or hygienist then smoothes the surface of the tooth root to make it harder for bacteria to stick in the future & easier for the gums to re-attach.\\\\nWhat Is Gum Disease?\\\\nCalled gingivitis in its early stages, periodontal disease is caused by excessive toxic bacteria (found in plaque) that attack your mouth\\\\u2019s soft tissues. We can help you keep your teeth & gums strong & disease-free with regular examinations & cleanings plus specialized interventions to treat periodontal disease if needed.\\\\nBenefits of Deep Cleanings\\\\nWhile deep cleanings cannot cure periodontal disease, it can slow or stop its progression. Deep cleanings can reduce the need for surgical intervention in the future & often helps swollen bleeding gums become healthy & pink again.\\\\nIf you have gum disease, we may recommend that deep cleanings, along with periodontal maintenance checkups, become part of your routine dental visits.\\\\nOverview of Periodontal Treatments\\\\nManagement of periodontal disease also involves a variety of other treatments in addition to deep cleanings. Periodontal therapy involves the enhancement of the gum tissue that holds your teeth in place. Diseased tissue must be replaced with healthy tissue whenever possible. If gum disease has caused severe gum recession, gum grafting may be necessary to move gum tissue back into place so that tooth roots are no longer exposed. We will also tackle the bacterial infection in your tissues directly by applying topical antibacterial or antibiotic solutions, either on their own or in conjunction with the treatment already mentioned.\\\\nPatients with very advanced or complicated periodontal disease may need to see a specialist. A periodontist is a dentist who works almost exclusively in the treatment of gingivitis, periodontal disease, & other afflictions of the mouth\\\\u2019s soft tissues. A periodontist is a fully trained dentist who completes additional specialized training in the field of periodontics after completing a general dental degree. While all dentists are trained to treat periodontal disease, this additional training makes a periodontist an expert in gum disease, its risk factors, & the symptoms & conditions that come with this dental disease. If you have been diagnosed with advanced gum disease, a periodontist may become a central part of your routine dental care & will work with us to create a treatment plan to keep your condition from getting worse.\\\", \\\"depth\\\": 0, \\\"source\\\": \\\"fineweb\\\", \\\"q_score\\\": 0.9966994063928446}\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.48}}"}

Now I'm calculating the training steps: with 200M tokens, a max length of 2048, and an effective batch size of 32,768 tokens per step, that comes out to roughly 6,100 steps total.

_source line 98_

已启动，汇报如下：

**实验**: `permuted_score_control_seed42`
- **GPU**: cuda:3 @ westd-41312, PID 962652
- **Hypothesis**: Permuted q-scores（将真实 q_score 随机打乱分配给样本）应该比 uniform 更差，证明 detector 的 provenance signal 是 adaptive weighting 的关键——而非仅仅权重的非均匀性在起作用。
- **数据**: `scored_data_random_shuffle.jsonl` (371,499 samples)，weighting_method=adaptive
- **配置**: 6103 steps, B=4, grad_accum=4, lr=5e-5, tau=0.8, n_min=2, seed=42
- **预计时长**: ~3h50m（~2.26s/step）
- **预期结果**: holdout PPL 应高于 uniform baseline (~17.25)，与 random_reweight 实验形成互补 ablation
